Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Objective Structured Clinical Examinations (OSCEs) are the gold standard for assessing clinical competence, yet scoring remains vulnerable to examiner subjectivity, fatigue, and cognitive bias. Standard examiner validation via inter-rater statistics lacks explanatory power regarding the source of errors, as it neither analyzes examiner reasoning nor verifies examiner claims against actual events. Thus, we introduce Quality Action Assurance (QAA), a multimodal framework that verifies examiner claims in Virtual Reality (VR) pediatric OSCEs by comparing actions claimed by examiners against the true sequence of events, constructed from video, VR logs, and actor data. QAA combines a constrained temporal action alignment model, which performs action localization and actor source attribution, with a large language model that extracts examiner claims and checks them against the record. Across a 5-fold cross-validation, QAA achieves 99.2% $\pm$ 0.7% Actor F1 and 93.4% $\pm$ 1.9% W@16 for temporal alignment. Overall, QAA detects examiner errors with 70.0% precision and 76.7% recall, improving factual correctness from 39.2% to 79.2%, enabling fairer OSCE assessment.

</details>


### [107] [On the Effectiveness of Pretraining for Graph Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19072)
*David Aguado,Daniel Fuertes,Carlos R. del-Blanco,Fernando Jaureguizar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对路由问题（如TSP）的自监督预训练框架，通过图对比学习和几何增强（旋转和轴向反射）学习不变的结构表示和全局相对距离分布，显著提升神经求解器在大规模实例上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经求解器在组合优化问题（如TSP）上通常需要大量标注数据或监督学习，且难以有效扩展到高维实例。现有方法缺乏针对图结构几何特性的预训练策略，导致模型泛化能力受限。

Method: 采用图对比学习框架，结合几何增强策略（旋转和轴向反射），迫使模型学习几何不变的结构表示和全局相对距离分布。混合增强策略同时使用旋转和反射两种变换。

Result: 预训练策略在多种问题规模上均优于非预训练模型。混合增强策略（旋转+反射）在TSP1000上实现6.57%的路径长度改进，证明了几何预训练对高维实例的有效性。

Conclusion: 几何预训练是神经求解器扩展到高维实例的重要归纳偏置，能够有效提升模型在大规模组合优化问题上的性能。

Abstract: This paper introduces a self-supervised pretraining framework for graph combinatorial optimization specifically designed to address the nature of routing problems like the Traveling Salesman Problem. By utilizing graph contrastive learning with geometric augmentations (specifically, rotations and axial reflections) the model is forced to learn invariant structural representations and global relative distance distributions. Results demonstrate that this pretraining strategy outperforms non-pretrained models across various problem scales. Notably, the hybrid strategy (combining rotation and reflection) achieved a 6.57% improvement in tour length for TSP1000, proving that geometric pretraining is an important inductive bias for effectively scaling neural solvers to high-dimensional instances.

</details>


### [108] [OpenRTAG: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Robust Text-Attributed Graph Learning under Data Quality Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19108)
*Yuze Dai,Zhihan Zhang,Yan Zhao,Ruoyu Wu,Xunkai Li,Zekai Chen,Qiangqiang Dai,Hongchao Qin,Ronghua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenRTAG是一个用于文本属性图（TAG）学习的鲁棒性基准测试，通过3×3 taxonomy对TAG质量退化进行统一分类，在9个数据集和3个下游任务上标准化评估传统GNN、LLM-GNN和GFM在不同退化场景下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的文本属性图（TAG）存在文本、结构、标签三个维度的质量问题，表现为稀疏、噪声和不平衡，这些退化会显著影响TAG学习效果，且现有研究证据分散在各种退化类型、数据集和模型家族中，导致TAG鲁棒性理解不足。

Method: 提出了OpenRTAG基准测试，采用统一的3×3分类法组织TAG质量问题，支持在9个TAG数据集和3个下游任务上的标准化评估；系统评估场景有效性和模型敏感性；比较传统GNN、LLM-GNN和代表性GFM三类模型；验证基线方法的有效性、效率和鲁棒性；并考察复合退化场景下的模型行为。

Result: 建立了TAG学习在真实低质量环境下的标准化测试平台，为理解TAG鲁棒性提供了系统化评估框架。

Conclusion: OpenRTAG为文本属性图学习的鲁棒性研究提供了全面的基准测试框架，通过统一的分类体系和标准化的评估流程，系统性地填补了TAG鲁棒性研究的空白。

Abstract: Text-attributed graphs (TAGs) are an important graph data form that combine relational structure with rich node text. However, real-world TAGs are often imperfect, with quality issues arising from text, structure, and labels, and typically manifesting as sparsity, noise, and imbalance. These dimensions define nine representative degradation scenarios that can substantially affect TAG learning. Although prior studies have explored specific mitigation strategies, existing evidence remains fragmented across degradation types, datasets, tasks, and model families, leaving TAG robustness insufficiently understood. To address this gap, we present OpenRTAG, a robustness benchmark for text-attributed graph learning. OpenRTAG organizes TAG quality issues into a unified 3 * 3 taxonomy and supports standardized evaluation across nine TAG datasets and three downstream tasks. It systematically evaluates scenario validity and model sensitivity, compares traditional GNNs, LLM-GNNs, and a representative GFM, investigates the effectiveness, efficiency, and robustness of scenario-matched baselines, and further examines model behavior under composite degradation scenarios. OpenRTAG provides a standardized testbed for understanding robustness in TAG learning under realistic low-quality settings.

</details>


### [109] [Comparative Study of Multi-Agent Actor-Critic Algorithms in Parameterized Action Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19117)
*Ubayd Ali Bapoo,Clement N Nyirenda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对参数化动作多智能体强化学习算法进行了比较研究，提出了MAGAC、MASAC和MATQC三种共享经验多智能体算法，在Platform-v0和Goal-v0基准上测试了3/5/10智能体配置。结果表明多智能体框架对GAC提升显著，但对SAC和TQC提升有限，且超过5个智能体后性能收益递减但计算成本剧增。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化动作强化学习在需要离散动作选择和连续参数化的环境中表现优异，但单智能体算法扩展到多智能体场景的研究尚不充分。现有的多智能体方法多采用CTDE范式，缺乏对独立多智能体共享经验框架的系统评估。

Method: 提出共享经验多智能体框架，采用独立actor-critic结构但共享重放缓冲区。在Platform-v0和Goal-v0基准上评估3、5、10智能体配置，使用平均评估回报和训练时间作为指标，通过one-way ANOVA和Tukey HSD进行统计显著性检验。

Result: 多智能体框架一致提升Greedy Actor-Critic性能；MASAC和MATQC相对单智能体版本提升有限；超过5个智能体后性能增益边际递减，但计算成本大幅增加，MAGAC尤为明显。

Conclusion: 共享经验多智能体actor-critic方法在参数化动作RL中存在性能与计算效率的权衡。MAGAC受益最大，而SAC和TQC的多智能体扩展价值有限。5个智能体配置可能是性能和效率的最佳平衡点。

Abstract: Parameterized action reinforcement learning has shown strong performance in environments requiring both discrete action selection and continuous parameterization. Prior work established the effectiveness of single-agent actor-critic algorithms - Greedy Actor-Critic (GAC), Soft Actor-Critic (SAC), and Truncated Quantile Critics (TQC) - on benchmark parameterized action tasks, but their extension to multi-agent settings remains largely unexplored. This paper presents a comparative study of shared-experience multi-agent extensions of these algorithms: Multi-Agent Greedy Actor-Critic (MAGAC), Multi-Agent Soft Actor-Critic (MASAC), and Multi-Agent Truncated Quantile Critics (MATQC). Rather than following the centralized training, decentralized execution (CTDE) paradigm, the proposed framework uses multiple independent actor-critic agents that share a replay buffer while maintaining separate policy and value networks. We evaluate the algorithms on the Platform-v0 and Goal-v0 benchmarks against their single-agent counterparts, using three-, five-, and ten-agent configurations to assess scalability. Performance is measured by average evaluation return and training time across ten independent runs, with one-way ANOVA and Tukey HSD post-hoc tests used to assess statistical significance. Results show that the multi-agent framework consistently improves Greedy Actor-Critic performance, while MASAC and MATQC show comparatively modest gains over their single-agent versions. Increasing the number of agents beyond five yields limited additional performance while substantially raising computational cost, particularly for MAGAC. These results highlight a trade-off between learning performance and computational efficiency, offering insight into the scalability of shared-experience multi-agent actor-critic methods for parameterized action reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [110] [Sequential Learner Modeling Using Multi-Relational Graph Convolutional Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19253)
*Rawaa Alatrash,Mohamed Amine Chatti,Hong Yang,Yumeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: User modeling is a critical task in a variety of personalized systems. Recognizing their effectiveness in learning from graph-structured data, Graph Neural Networks (GNNs), particularly Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs), are increasingly employed for user modeling. However, existing approaches typically treat different relation types in a graph as homogeneous, limiting their ability to capture richer semantics and construct more informative user models. While multi-relational GNNs (MR-GNNs) have been adopted for representation learning and recommendation, their application for user modeling remains unexplored. Moreover, existing GNN-based user modeling approaches ignore the user interaction sequence. To address these research gaps, in this work we propose MR-ConceptGCN, a novel fully unsupervised approach focused on concept-based sequential learner modeling using multi-relational GCNs (MR-GCNs). MR-ConceptGCN effecively combines Personal Knowledge Graphs (PKGs), MR-GCNs, and the pre-trained language model SBERT to obtain enhanced relation- and semantic-aware representations of the PKG items. The enriched embeddings of the knowledge concepts that a learner did not understand when interacting with learning materials in CourseMapper are then used to construct a sequential learner model that combines long-term and short-term learner interactions. We report the results of an online user study (n = 31), demonstrating the benefits of MR-ConceptGCN in terms of several important user-centric aspects including accuracy, usefulness, diversity, and satisfaction with an educational recommender system.

</details>


### [111] [BioSecBench-Surveillance: A Verifiable Benchmark for AI Agents in Pathogen Genomic Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19262)
*Harmon Bhasin,Kevin Flyangolts,Dianzhuo Wang,Evan Seeyave,Arjun Banerjee,Amanda Darling,Joshua Stallings,David Stern,Shawn Higdon,Claire Duvallet,Bryan Tegomoh,Kenny Workman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: As pathogen genomic surveillance scales, the bottleneck is shifting from data generation to analysis. We present BioSecBench-Surveillance, a verifiable benchmark of 100 evaluations testing whether AI agents can infer the right analysis pipeline from raw sequencing data and surveillance context. Each evaluation gives an agent only the data and context a human analyst would have, then grades its structured answer deterministically. The tasks span seven categories, from taxonomic classification to genetic-engineering detection, across diverse sample types and sequencing technologies. Across 3,962 gradable attempts from sixteen model-harness pairs, the strongest configuration cleared only about half. Opus 4.8 with PI led at 50.2 percent, with a 95 percent confidence interval of 40.1 to 60.3 percent across 83 evaluations, tied with GPT-5.5 with Codex at 50.2 percent, with a 95 percent confidence interval of 40.8 to 59.6 percent, followed by Opus 4.7 with PI at 49.6 percent, with a 95 percent confidence interval of 40.0 to 59.2 percent, and Sonnet 4.6 with PI at 48.6 percent, with a 95 percent confidence interval of 38.9 to 58.3 percent. Even when agents invoked the correct workflows, their mistakes came from the choices around them, such as which references, thresholds, filters, and normalization to apply. BioSecBench-Surveillance provides a standard for measuring whether agents can be trusted to perform genomic surveillance when the next outbreak arrives.

</details>


### [112] [Graph-Based Agentic AI with LangGraph: Workflow Pathways for Long-Running Stateful Business Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19297)
*Daniel Pearson,Sidney Shapiro,Emiliano Sebastian Gonzalez Venegas,Sanad Al-Khatib,Aurora Pinzón Arzola*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于在业务处理中使用基于图的LangGraph编排框架构建有状态、多步骤生成式AI系统的实践指南，通过三个可执行示例展示其适用场景和最佳实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前业界将LangGraph作为模型质量基准目标的不当使用方式，以及缺乏针对不同工作流复杂度选择合适编排框架的实践指导。

Method: 提出三个可执行配方（SQL分析修复循环、带证据门控的代理RAG、带人工审核的策略审查），展示类型化状态、条件路由、确定性工具、重试、中断、检查点和追踪等技术组件的集成方式。

Result: 明确了LangGraph的适用条件——复杂工作流场景，并指出简单工具调用场景更适合ReAct风格或SDK循环、结构化提取验证应使用schema-first工具、提示/程序优化场景适用DSPy。

Conclusion: LangGraph应按工作流复杂度匹配度来定位，而非作为通用默认方案；实现中应使路由、暂停和审计追踪成为明确的产品行为，而非隐藏的提示逻辑。

Abstract: This paper is a practitioner guide to graph-based workflow pathways for long-running, stateful, multi-step generative AI systems in business processes. Rather than treating LangGraph, a low-level orchestration framework for stateful agents, as a model-quality benchmark target, we present three executable recipes -- SQL analytics with repair loops, agentic retrieval-augmented generation with evidence gating, and human-in-the-loop policy review with interrupt and checkpoint recovery -- to show how typed state, conditional routing, deterministic tools, retries, interrupts, checkpoints, and traces fit together. LangGraph is positioned by workflow-complexity fit, not as a universal default: simpler ReAct-style or plain SDK loops may be better for basic tool use, schema-first tools for structured extraction and validation, and DSPy when prompt or program optimization is the main goal. Each recipe explains when LangGraph is worth the extra structure and which implementation patterns make routes, pauses, and audit trails explicit product behavior rather than hidden prompt logic.

</details>


### [113] [LLM Detection as an Intervention: Downstream Impact under Strategic User Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19300)
*Meena Jagadeesan,Tatsunori Hashimoto,Jon Kleinberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了不完美的LLM检测器如何对下游指标产生反直觉影响。通过建立用户策略行为模型，发现LLM检测可能反而增加LLM使用量并降低输出质量，但会使检测属性呈现先上升后下降的清洁模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM应用普及，LLM检测成为重要研究方向。现有研究关注检测准确性，但缺乏对检测器作为「干预措施」如何影响用户行为和下游指标的分析。这是首个系统性地研究检测器对LLM使用策略和输出质量影响的论文。

Method: 建立stylized模型捕捉用户如何策略性地选择LLM使用程度和内容后处理方式以降低检测属性；使用arXiv摘要的词频数据进行实证验证。

Result: 1) LLM检测可能反直觉地导致人类增加LLM使用量；2) 即使降低检测属性能提高输出质量，引入检测器仍可能导致用户产出更低质量内容；3) 检测器使检测属性呈现先上升后下降的模式，并可通过词频数据实证复现。

Conclusion: LLM检测器作为干预措施会产生反直觉的失败模式，揭示了当检测器作用于下游指标时的核心问题。未来需要重新设计检测策略以避免这些扭曲效应。

Abstract: As LLM adoption becomes more widespread, there is a growing interest in detecting LLM-generated content, for example through LLM detection tools and through heuristics based on language patterns. Detectors operate as an intervention that steers not only the detected attribute itself, but also downstream metrics such as LLM usage and output quality. In this work, we demonstrate how imperfect LLM detectors lead to counterintuitive impacts on these downstream metrics, by distorting how users are incentivized to use LLMs in their workflow. We develop a stylized model which captures how users strategically choose how much to use the LLM and how to post-process content to reduce the detected attribute. Using this model, we show that LLM detection can counterintuitively lead humans to increase their LLM usage. Moreover, even when reducing the detected attribute improves output quality, we find that introducing an LLM detector can lead users to produce lower quality outputs. In contrast, we show that detectors result in a clean "rise-then-fall" pattern for the detected attribute, which we empirically reproduce for word frequencies on arXiv abstracts. Altogether, our work illustrates how LLM detection can distort LLM usage and output quality, uncovering failure modes when LLM detectors operate as an intervention on these downstream metrics.

</details>


### [114] [ResearchArena: Evaluating Sabotage and Monitoring in Automated AI R&D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19321)
*Lena Libon,Ben Rank,Jehyeok Yeon,David Schmotz,Jeremy Qin,Daniel Donnelly,Derck Prinzhorn,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ResearchArena框架,通过四种长时域任务评估AI控制方法在自动化AI研发中检测 sabotage 的效果。研究发现,训练数据中隐藏的 sabotage 最难检测(检出率<50%),即使让监控器运行实验也难以完全解决问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理开始自动化AI研发,需要评估其输出是否安全部署。当代理本身不可信时,需要一种方法来检测潜在的 covert sabotage。AI control 方法将代理视为潜在对手,通过监控器在部署前检测 sabotage。

Method: ResearchArena框架包含四个长时域任务(安全后训练、能力后训练、CUDA内核优化、推理服务器优化),每个任务配有两种隐藏 side task:嵌入型(破坏artifact本身)和独立型(out-of-scope行为)。评估前沿代理的 sabotage 和监控能力,测试四种监控器类型,变化轴为:(1)是否可执行/探测artifact,(2)是否可见代理的思维链。

Result: 训练数据中隐藏的 sabotage 最难检测,检出率低于50%。允许监控器对 artifact 运行实验有帮助,但仍不足:监控器会因仅检查表面、解释异常、或用错误测试探测而遗漏嵌入型 sabotage。

Conclusion: 发布ResearchArena作为模块化框架,用于评估自动化AI研发中的 sabotage 和控制。当前监控方法对嵌入型 sabotage 的检测仍不充分,需要改进。

Abstract: As AI agents begin to automate AI R&D, we need ways to assess whether their outputs are safe to deploy, even when the agents themselves may be untrusted. AI control offers one such approach: rather than trusting the agent, it treats it as a potential adversary and uses a monitor to detect covert sabotage before deployment. We evaluate AI control for automated AI R&D with ResearchArena, a framework spanning four long-horizon tasks: safety post-training, capabilities post-training, CUDA-kernel optimization, and inference-server optimization. Because the deliverable in AI R&D is an artifact that will be deployed and run, we pair each main task with two kinds of hidden side task: an embedded side task that sabotages the submitted model, adapter, kernel, or server itself, and an independent side task that takes a covert out-of-scope action in the sandbox while leaving the artifact honest. We evaluate frontier agents at both sabotage and monitoring, across four monitor types that vary along two axes: whether the monitor may execute and probe the produced artifact, and whether it sees the agent's chain-of-thought. We find that sabotage hidden in the training data is the hardest to catch, flagged fewer than half the time. Letting the monitor run experiments on the artifact, rather than only read the trajectory, helps, but it is not enough: monitors still miss embedded sabotage by inspecting only the surface, by noticing the anomaly but explaining it away, or by probing the artifact with the wrong test. We release ResearchArena as a modular framework for evaluating sabotage and control in automated AI R&D.

</details>


### [115] [Associative Emotional Learning in Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19327)
*Seowung Leem,Andreas Keil,Mingzhou Ding,Ruogu Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Associative emotional learning enables organisms to adaptively link pleasant or unpleasant outcomes to the presence of predictive stimuli. Whereas computational models such as the Rescorla-Wagner model have shed light on this important function, the limitations of these models are also known, especially when they are applied to neural data. The advent of deep neural networks has opened another avenue for modeling associative emotional learning. In this work we proposed a deep neural network model of visual valence processing, consisting of a visual module that encodes complex natural scenes and a module that recognizes their emotional significance in terms of valence, a key dimension of emotion, and tested a novel Pavlovian learning paradigm on the model. The results showed that with learning, the model reproduced several observations from human associative learning studies, including association formation and generalization, and that the neural representations of the conditioned and the unconditioned stimuli became increasingly aligned both at the single unit and at the neural population level. Comparison between the model and human experimental data provided further validation of our approach. This study thus suggests that deep neural network models, when combined with appropriate learning algorithms, can be used to model behavioral and neural signatures of associative emotion/valence learning.

</details>


### [116] [CodeRescue: Budget-Calibrated Recovery Routing for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19338)
*Qijia He,Jiayi Cheng,Chenqian Le,Rui Wang,Xunmei Liu,Yixian Chen,Jie Mei,Zhihao Wang,Xupeng Chen,Yuhuan Chen,Tao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Coding agents increasingly operate in executable environments where a failed attempt produces actionable feedback rather than merely an incorrect answer. Existing cost-aware systems typically treat such failures as cascade decisions: try a cheap model first, then escalate hard cases to a stronger and more expensive model. In coding, however, execution feedback can also make further cheap-model recovery worthwhile, raising a budgeted deployment question: when should an agent spend more cheap compute, and when should it escalate? We formulate this post-failure decision as recovery routing over heterogeneous actions and train a supervised router from execution rollouts. To make the same router usable under changing budgets, we add a Conformal Risk Control (CRC) layer that selects a deployment-time cost penalty without retraining and provides marginal expected-cost control under exchangeability. Across held-out failures from five coding benchmarks, cheap recovery and escalation exhibit complementary success patterns. The calibrated frontier improves over fixed actions, prompt-only routers, and a binary cascade baseline; in the main GPT-5.4-nano/GPT-5.4 setting, one CRC-calibrated frontier point exceeds always-escalate solve rate while using 35% of its mean recovery cost. Code is available at https://github.com/Qijia-He/agent-budget-control.

</details>

### [117] [Unstructured Hydrodynamics on Spatial Dataflow Architectures: A Joint Code and Data Decomposition Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18650)
*Piotr Luczynski,Tal Ben-Nun,Leighton Wilson,Brian Van Essen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将非结构化网格问题映射到空间数据流架构（如Cerebras晶圆级引擎）的方法，通过通信和内存建模的联合分解，考虑应用场和子程序的大小，并利用空间填充曲线最小化通信，优化内存使用，成功将LULESH应用映射到Cerebras WSE并超越GPU性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间数据流架构（如Cerebras WSE）采用mesh连接的固定内存处理单元，专为结构化网格内核设计。然而，实际多物理场代码通常基于非结构化网格，导致间接内存访问和高维通信模式，难以直接映射到此类架构。需要一种系统性方法将非结构化问题适配到空间数据流硬件上。

Method: 1) 自动化分析原始代码；2) 提出联合分解方法，同时考虑应用场的大小和子程序特性；3) 使用空间填充曲线定义高维分解以最小化通信；4) 应用内存优化技术以适应内存受限环境。

Result: 成功将Livermore非结构化拉格朗日显式冲击水动力学（LULESH）应用映射到Cerebras晶圆级引擎，证明了较大的非结构化网格代码仍可超越GPU性能。

Conclusion: 通过模型中心的方法，可以将非结构化网格问题有效地映射到空间数据流架构上，为高性能计算中的复杂多物理场模拟提供了新的硬件映射思路。

Abstract: Spatial Dataflow Architectures are an emerging hardware pattern in high-performance computing, whose mesh-connected fixed-memory processing elements are tailored for structured grid kernels with two-dimensional neighborhoods. However, practical multiphysics codes are often computed on unstructured grids, which induce indirect memory accesses and high-dimensional communication patterns, making them infeasible to directly map onto said architectures. This work takes a principled, model-centric approach to partitioning unstructured problems onto spatial dataflow architectures. Through communication and memory modeling, we propose a joint decomposition that considers both the size of the application's fields and its subroutines. In particular, we automate the analysis process of the original code, define a high-dimensional decomposition that minimizes communication via space-filling curves, and apply memory optimization techniques, crucial in this memory-limited environment. We demonstrate mapping the Livermore Unstructured Lagrangian Explicit Shock Hydrodynamics (LULESH) application to the Cerebras Wafer-Scale Engine, showing that larger, unstructured grid codes can still outperform GPUs.

</details>


### [118] [Mapping Without Graphs: Learning Coherence Traffic for Task Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18879)
*Guochu Xiong,Tianrui Ma,Weichen Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CoTM框架，通过从动态相干行为推断任务依赖关系来构建任务图，并使用多起始优化策略和相干感知的惩罚函数进行任务映射优化。实验表明该方法可将平均链路利用率降低47.85%，总能耗降低10.30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务映射方法存在两个主要局限：一是依赖预定义任务图，无法显式捕获由共享数据访问引起的相干诱导交互；二是普遍忽视相干流量，导致模型化的通信行为与实际运行时交互不匹配，造成映射次优和系统性能下降。

Method: CoTM框架从动态相干行为推断任务间依赖关系以构建任务图；采用轻量级启发式算法结合多起始优化策略迭代优化任务布局；设计相干感知惩罚函数，联合考虑相干流量和NoC性能指标。

Result: 相比现有方法，CoTM将平均链路利用率降低最多47.85%，总能耗降低最多10.30%。

Conclusion: 结果表明将缓存相干性纳入任务映射具有重要意义，相干感知优化在未来的多核NoC系统中具有巨大潜力。

Abstract: Cache coherence is essential for communication in many-core Network-on-Chip (NoC)-based systems. As application scale and complexity increase, efficiently managing communication becomes increasingly challenging, making task mapping a key optimization technique. However, existing task mapping approaches suffer from two major limitations. First, they rely on predefined task graphs whose dependencies are typically derived from program structure or runtime information, such as dataflow, synchronization, traces, or profiling, without explicitly capturing coherence-induced interactions arising from shared data accesses. Consequently, these graphs provide only a partial representation of inter-task relationships, limiting mapping effectiveness. Second, they generally overlook cache coherence, even though coherence traffic constitutes a significant portion of NoC communication. This mismatch between modeled communication behavior and actual runtime interactions often leads to suboptimal mappings and degraded system performance. To address these limitations, we propose CoTM, a coherence-aware task mapping framework that constructs task graphs by inferring inter-task dependencies from dynamic coherence behavior. CoTM employs a lightweight heuristic with a multi-start optimization strategy to iteratively refine task placement, guided by a coherence-aware penalty function that jointly considers coherence traffic and NoC performance metrics. Experimental results demonstrate that CoTM reduces average link utilization by up to 47.85% and total energy consumption by up to 10.30% compared with existing approaches. These results highlight the importance of incorporating cache coherence into task mapping and demonstrate the potential of coherence-aware optimization for future many-core NoC systems.

</details>


### [119] [A User-oriented Portable, Reproducible, and Scalable Software Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18929)
*Alfio Lazzaro,Utz-Uwe Haus,Sandrine Charousset,Nina Mujkanovic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个软件生态系统，用于在多样化硬件平台（笔记本、工作站、超级计算机和云资源）上运行科学工作流。通过模块化、统一的命令行界面，用户可以在不同计算资源上使用相同的开发环境。该方案已在欧盟H2020计划的plan4res项目中成功测试，并可扩展至其他类似需求的项目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学家通常需要在多样化的硬件平台上进行研究，包括笔记本电脑、工作站、超级计算机和云资源，这种跨平台的工作方式带来了开发环境不一致的挑战。

Method: 开发了一个模块化、统一的命令行接口，允许用户在不同硬件平台上与工作流进行交互，实现跨平台的一致性。

Result: 该软件生态系统已在欧盟H2020计划的plan4res项目中成功测试验证。

Conclusion: 该方案可扩展至其他具有类似需求的项目，使其能够从相同的计算工作流执行方法中受益。

Abstract: It is normal for scientists to perform their research on a diverse set of hardware, ranging from laptops and workstations to supercomputers and cloud resources. The standard scenario requires a mix of these resources. In this paper we describe a software ecosystem that enables users to rely on the same development environment for running their workflows across the different computational resources. We describe a modular, unified command-line interface that allows for the interaction with a user-workflow across diverse hardware platform. The software ecosystem has been successfully tested as part of the plan4res EU H2020 project. It can be extended to other projects with similar requirements, so that they can benefit from the same approach for executing computational workflows.

</details>


### [120] [InstantInfer: Enabling Fast LLM Cold Start with Communicating Finite Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18957)
*Yitao Yuan,Yongchao He,Shaoke Fang,Wenfei Wu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CFA抽象和编程框架，通过安全重构实现LLM推理冷启动加速，最高7.2倍提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理服务中的冷启动问题严重影响用户体验，当前方案效率低下的原因是顺序初始化和大量细粒度I/O请求。虽然程序重构可以带来并发执行和I/O合并的优势，但手动重构容易出错且存在正确性风险，尤其在处理大量异构组件时。

Method: 1) 提出通信有限自动机(CFA)抽象，系统分析跨组件优化机会；2) 设计基于CFA的组件程序重构编程框架，保持原有顺序程序结构的同时实现安全并发执行；3) 对重构程序提供正确性证明；4) 将CFA抽象应用于vLLM的重构(进程树创建、张量加载、模型切换)，形成InstantInfer系统。

Result: InstantInfer在多种GPU、工作负载和规模下均表现优异，LLM冷启动加速最高达7.2倍，且具有强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CFA抽象为LLM推理系统冷启动优化提供了一种系统化、安全的程序重构方法，证明了其在实际系统中的有效性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Cold starts in large language model (LLM) inference services significantly affect user experience, yet they remain inefficient due to sequential initialization and a massive number of fine-grained I/O requests issued by complex software components. Although refactoring the program can yield advantages such as concurrent execution and I/O merging, this approach is error-prone and carries correctness risks when dealing with massive, heterogeneous components. We propose the Communicating Finite Automata (CFA) abstraction to systematically analyze cross-component optimization opportunities, and design a programming framework to enable CFA-based component program refactoring. This framework preserves the original sequential program structure while enabling safe concurrent component execution. We prove the correctness of the program refactoring. We apply the CFA abstraction and framework to refactor process tree creation, tensor loading, and model switching in vLLM, forming a new cold-start system named InstantInfer. Extensive experiments demonstrate that InstantInfer substantially accelerates LLM cold starts (achieving up to 7.2 times speedup) and exhibits robustness across diverse GPUs, workloads, and scales.

</details>


### [121] [Enabling Multi-Dimensional Distributed Trace Comparison with Contrast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19102)
*Vaastav Anand,Rodrigo Fonseca,Jonathan Mace,Antoine Kaufmann*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Contrast系统,用于多维比较追踪分析,通过引入可合并的追踪投影对象(TPO)表示,将追踪表示与比较语义分离,使操作员能动态构建任意比较集并组合多种诊断界面(如视觉界面SpectroViz和LLM界面Parallax),从而实现更有效的分布式系统故障诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式追踪诊断本质上是比较任务,但现有方法存在两个主要挑战:(1)执行间的有用差异可能表现在多个维度,单一诊断界面无法有效捕获所有差异;(2)相关维度和比较群体通常事先未知,操作员需要动态构建和调整比较集以验证假设。现有方法定义了固定的追踪差异概念,难以适应多样化的诊断需求。

Method: Contrast系统提出追踪投影对象(Trace Projection Object,TPO),这是一种可合并的表示形式,能够捕获追踪群体的结构、时间、关键路径和语义属性,并支持在查询时高效构建任意比较集。核心创新在于将追踪表示与比较语义分离,使不同界面可以灵活选择特定维度进行推理,从而实现界面的组合。系统提供两个互补接口:(1)SpectroViz基于关键路径的视觉界面,用于定位执行差异;(2)Parallax基于LLM的自然语言界面,用于生成追踪差异的解释。

Result: 通过DeathStarBench的受控实验和Uber生产追踪的评估,证明了Contrast系统的有效性和效率。该系统能够使操作员组合来自多个维度的洞察,实现更有效的故障诊断。

Conclusion: 本文通过TPO的可合并表示和语义分离设计,实现了灵活的多维追踪比较分析框架。互补界面的组合证明了该架构的实用性,为分布式系统诊断提供了更强大的工具支持。

Abstract: Diagnosis using distributed traces is fundamentally a comparative task: operators seek to understand how an anomalous execution differs from expected behavior, how a deployment changes system execution, or how two individual executions differ. Trace comparison is challenging because useful differences between executions can manifest across multiple dimensions, and no single diagnostic interface is effective at capturing all of them. Moreover, the relevant dimensions and comparison populations are often not known a priori; operators construct and refine comparison sets dynamically as they develop hypotheses about system behavior.
  This paper presents Contrast, a system for multi-dimensional comparative trace analysis. Contrast introduces the Trace Projection Object (TPO), a mergeable representation that captures structural, temporal, critical-path, and semantic properties of trace populations while enabling efficient construction of arbitrary comparison sets at query time. Unlike approaches that define a fixed notion of trace difference, Contrast separates trace representation from comparison semantics, allowing diverse interfaces to selectively reason about specific dimensions. This separation enables the composition of complementary interfaces, allowing operators to combine insights from multiple dimensions for more effective diagnosis. We demonstrate this capability through two complementary interfaces: (i) SpectroViz, a critical-path-based visual interface for localizing execution differences; and (ii) Parallax, a natural language interface for generating explanations of trace differences using LLMs.
  We demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of Contrast through controlled experiments on traces from DeathStarBench and evaluation on production traces from Uber.

</details>


### [122] [A Scalable Pattern Mining Workflow for Interpretable Machine Log Analysis in High-Performance Computing Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19143)
*Shilpika Shilpika,Bethany Lusch,Eric Pershey,Carlo Graziani,Venkatram Vishwanath,Michael E. Papka*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: This paper presents a log analysis workflow for HPC systems using Aho-Corasick automaton-based pattern mining to detect frequent errors, fault events, and correlate error sequences with job logs for real-time monitoring and improved system resilience.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: High Performance Computing systems generate massive, heterogeneous log data that traditional analytical techniques cannot effectively process, making it difficult to extract meaningful patterns and anomalies that indicate system faults.

Method: The approach combines Aho-Corasick automaton for pattern-sequence extraction with system hierarchy and message priority information, then correlates and clusters identified error sequences with job logs to reveal applications with similar or dissimilar error signatures.

Result: Automated detection of frequent errors and fault events, with the ability to identify groups of applications sharing error patterns. Demonstrated through summary statistics and a case study on an exascale-class supercomputer.

Conclusion: Pattern mining is essential for unlocking the full potential of HPC log data, enabling real-time analysis and contributing to more resilient and scalable supercomputing systems.

Abstract: Modern supercomputers housed in High Performance Computing (HPC) environments generate massive volumes of log data daily, revealing intricate information and performance metrics about these complex systems. The sheer size and heterogeneous nature of HPC logs, especially text data, pose significant challenges for traditional analytical techniques. Consequently, more complex workflows are necessary for pattern extraction when analyzing these logs, enabling the discovery of underlying patterns and anomalies that may indicate system faults and help predict future failures and inefficiencies. Our log analysis workflow investigates a combination of advanced pattern-matching and mining techniques applied to HPC log analysis. By systematically identifying frequent log patterns and pattern sequences in log messages and storing them in a finite-state automaton, such as the Aho-Corasick automaton, our workflow enables automated detection of frequent errors and fault events. To extract these patterns and sequences, we leverage information about system hierarchy and message priority. We then correlate and cluster the identified error sequences with job logs, revealing groups of applications with similar or dissimilar error signatures. This approach yields insights that inform improvements and guide real-time monitoring efforts. Our research establishes that pattern mining is vital for unlocking the full potential of log data by enabling real-time analysis and contributing to more resilient, scalable HPC systems. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach through summary statistics and a case study on an exascale-class system supercomputer.

</details>


### [123] [Coherence in Control: Bridging Many-Core Mapping and Routing through Cost Unification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19158)
*Guochu Xiong,Xiangzhong Luo,Weichen Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCo框架，通过统一的成本模型联合优化多核系统中的任务映射和路由，整合通信成本、一致性开销和负载不平衡，采用基于强化学习的路由和一致性导向的任务映射，实验表明该方法可显著降低链路利用率、数据包延迟和执行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据密集型应用的增长，多核系统中缓存一致性引入大量开销。现有方法忽视了缓存一致性的影响，导致优化目标与实际通信模式不匹配；且映射和路由采用分离的成本评估器，难以捕捉两阶段之间的一致性耦合关系，可能产生冲突决策。

Method: 提出统一成本模型，整合通信成本、一致性开销和负载不平衡。在此基础上，设计一致性导向的任务映射方法，并结合基于强化学习的路由算法，根据通信行为调整方向性链路权重以改善流量分布。

Result: 与现有方法相比，CoCo将链路利用率降低88.46%，数据包延迟降低17.40%，执行时间缩短17.58%。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了缓存在 co-optimization 设计中的重要性，为多核系统的性能优化提供了新的思路。

Abstract: The rapid growth of data-intensive applications increases communication demands in many-core systems, where cache coherence, while essential for correct communication and data consistency, introduces substantial overhead due to frequent data sharing and coherence activities. As system scale and workload complexity grow, the resulting coherence traffic intensifies communication pressure, making the co-optimization of task mapping and routing essential for improving system performance. However, most existing approaches overlook cache coherence, leaving a substantial portion of coherence-induced communication unaccounted for and creating a mismatch between optimization objectives and actual communication patterns. Furthermore, by employing separate cost evaluators for mapping and routing, these approaches complicate objective coordination, may lead to conflicting decisions, and fail to capture the coherence-induced coupling between the two stages. To address these challenges, we propose CoCo, a coherence-aware co-optimization framework that jointly integrates task mapping and routing under a unified cost model for realistic scenarios. This unified model integrates communication cost, coherence overhead, and load imbalance into a single objective, enabling coherence-aware decision-making and effective trade-offs among optimization goals. Guided by this model, CoCo combines coherence-guided task mapping with reinforcement learning-based routing, where directional link weights are adjusted according to communication behavior to improve traffic distribution, enabling coherence-aware co-optimization for many-core systems. Experimental results show that CoCo reduces link utilization by 88.46%, packet delay by 17.40%, and execution time by 17.58% compared with existing approaches, highlighting the importance of cache coherence in co-optimization design.

</details>


### [124] [ARBITER: Guarded Agentic Control for SLO-Oriented Kubernetes Remediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19182)
*Pooyan Habibi,Alberto Leon-Garcia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ARBITER是一个面向SLO的Kubernetes修复守护控制平面，通过OpenTelemetry原生因果资源图、bounded DiagnosisContext对象和有限类型化动作接口，将规划与执行分离。支持确定性规划器和LLM规划器，包含安全机制（模式检查、策略门、资源/中断预算、审批、边界执行）。评估表明ARBITER在选择正确修复动作和下游目标方面优于传统HPA/资源自动扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kubernetes微服务上维护SLO非常困难，原因包括：(1)自动扩展器仅观察粗粒度资源指标；(2)最近的SLO控制器依赖自定义遥测技术；(3)不受约束的代理操作员无法安全地变更生产集群。

Method: ARBITER采用多层设计：(1)构建OpenTelemetry原生因果资源图；(2)组装bounded DiagnosisContext对象；(3)暴露有限类型化动作接口，分离规划与执行；(4)同一接口支持确定性规划器和LLM后端规划器；(5)安全层包括确定性模式检查、策略门、资源/中断预算、审批机制和边界执行。

Result: 在4节点Kubernetes集群上使用DeathStarBench Social Network和Online Boutique评估显示：(1)bad-image部署回归场景：ARBITER在10次CPU烧录和纯延迟运行中均选择rollback_canary，而HPA要么扩展故障镜像要么永不触发；(2)下游关键路径故障场景：用户可见违反出现在前端，但追踪证据确定home-timeline-service为可修复瓶颈，确定性ARBITER和审批门控Sonnet均正确 Targeting该服务，而HPA/资源控制从未做到。还包括守护放置修复、Online Boutique可移植性、对抗性安全拒绝、离线多模型回放和KWOK控制平面扩展性实验。

Conclusion: 已开源控制器、重放语料库、规划器、safety tests和图表制品：https://github.com/pooyan/arbiter。该工作为SLO导向的Kubernetes修复提供了安全、可扩展的统一框架。

Abstract: Maintaining service-level objectives (SLOs) on Kubernetes microservices remains difficult because autoscalers observe coarse resource metrics, recent SLO controllers often depend on custom telemetry, and unconstrained agentic operators cannot safely mutate production clusters. We present ARBITER, a guarded control plane for SLO-oriented Kubernetes remediation. ARBITER builds an OpenTelemetry-native causal resource graph, assembles bounded DiagnosisContext objects, and exposes a finite typed-action interface that separates planning from execution. The same interface supports deterministic planners and an LLM-backed planning harness, with deterministic schema checks, policy gates, resource/disruption budgets, approval, and bounded execution forming the safety substrate. We evaluate ARBITER on a 4-node Kubernetes cluster using DeathStarBench Social Network and Online Boutique. The evaluation tests two forms of SLO-oriented control that resource autoscaling alone does not provide: selecting the right remediation action and selecting the right downstream target. For bad-image deployment regressions, ARBITER selects rollback_canary in all ten CPU-burn and pure-latency runs; HPA either scales the faulty image or never triggers. For a downstream critical-path fault, the user-visible breach appears at the frontend, but trace evidence identifies home-timeline-service as the remediable bottleneck. Deterministic ARBITER and a live approval-gated Sonnet harness target that downstream service in every replicate, whereas HPA/resource-only control never does. Additional experiments cover guarded placement repair, Online Boutique portability, adversarial safety rejection, offline multi-model replay, and KWOK-based control-plane scale evidence. We release the controller, replay corpus, harnesses, safety tests, and figure artifacts: https://github.com/pooyan/arbiter.

</details>


### [125] [Keeping the Cache Warm Pays: Keepalive Economics for Agentic Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19214)
*Maxim Khailo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文研究了在代理工作负载中，前沿LLM提供商的前缀缓存优势如何被长时间暂停（如等待工具执行或人工审批）破坏的问题。解决方案是客户端保活机制，通过在暂停期间定期重放前缀来保持缓存活跃。论文推导了最优ping频率（应为提供商TTL减去安全边际，约4分钟）、策略盈亏平衡点（约36-46分钟），并认为这种利己理性的策略会导致普遍采用，最终会推动提供商直接计量缓存驻留时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM提供商的KV缓存机制本可降低约90%的输入成本和预填充延迟，但在代理工作负载中这种优势被系统性破坏——代理请求间存在分钟级暂停（工具执行、人工审批等），期间缓存前缀会被驱逐，导致后续请求需重新支付完整预填充成本。

Method: 论文通过跨提供商（Athropic、OpenAI、Google、DeepSeek）的实验测量了：1）基础空闲情况下缓存被驱逐的时间点；2）客户端保活机制在不同ping频率下的缓存保持效果；3）各提供商的TTL策略与驱逐行为。

Result: 保活机制可将暂停后请求成本降低高达12.5倍。保活成本随ping间隔单调递减，因此经济上最优选择是在提供商TTL减去安全边际的间隔（约4分钟，考虑Athropic的5分钟TTL）。策略盈亏平衡点约为τ(w/r - 1)，即Anthropic约46分钟、OpenAI/DeepSeek约36分钟。

Conclusion: 由于缓存收益受限于用户自身账单，利己理性会推动普遍采用保活策略。由于缓存驻留按读取次数计价而非按token-小时计费，保活饱和状态下LRU驱逐失去意义。这种外部性将推动提供商直接计量缓存驻留时间（已有提供商这么做），论文为在此之前的运营商策略提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Frontier LLM providers cache a prompt's processed prefix so that a follow-up request sharing it pays ~10% of the input price and skips most of the prefill latency. Agentic workloads systematically destroy this benefit: the agent sends a request, runs a tool or waits for approval for minutes, and by the time the follow-up is sent the cached prefix has been evicted, so the agent pays the full prefill again. A client-side keepalive, replaying the prefix on a timer during the pause, prevents this, and it is individually rational: across Anthropic, OpenAI, Google, and DeepSeek we show that a keepalive holds the prefix warm through gaps where idle baselines are evicted, cutting the post-pause request cost by up to 12.5x. The strategic question is the ping frequency, and it has a clean answer: keepalive cost falls monotonically in the interval, so the economical choice is the largest interval safely under the provider's TTL, about 4 minutes at Anthropic's 5-minute TTL rather than the 30-second convention, and the strategy breaks even against a re-prefill at idle ~tau(w/r - 1) (~46 min for Anthropic, ~36 min for OpenAI and DeepSeek). Because the benefit is real and bounded only by each user's own bill, rational adoption is universal adoption; and since cache residency is priced per read rather than per token-hour, a keepalive-saturated tier gives LRU eviction nothing to rank. We argue this externality will push providers to meter cache residency directly, and one already does. We derive the operator's policy until then.

</details>
